According to Koi Security, a legitimate-looking developer managed to slip in rogue code within an npm package called " postmark-mcp " that copied an official Postmark Labs library of the same name. The malicious functionality was introduced in version 1.0.16, which was released on September 17, 2025.  The actual "postmark-mcp" library, available on GitHub , exposes an MCP server  to allow users to send emails, access and use email templates, and track campaigns using artificial intelligence (AI) assistants.
